In a 5-fold cross-validation setting, X-LMC yields higher point estimates than static architectures and spatiotemporal baselines adapted from related angiographic tasks, achieving a Quadratic Weighted Kappa (QWK) of 0.398 (vs. 0.322) and a dichotomized macro-F1 score of 0.711 (vs. 0.663) against the best-performing baseline. X-LMC performance also aligns with the observed clinical inter-rater agreement (QWK: 0.314). As the first DSA study attempting to automate LMC scoring, we demonstrate that multi-view temporal deep learning can capture collateral-specific contrast kinetics. Ultimately, these benchmarks delineate the clinical ambiguities and achievable performance boundaries of automated ASITN/SIR grading, establishing a reproducible foundation for objective hemodynamic phenotyping in stroke cohorts. Code is available at https://github.com/maedehafezi/X-LMC.

</details>


### [69] [ForeSightGuide: An Anticipatory Framework toward Accurate and Low-Redundancy Guidance for the Visually Impaired](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18993)
*Zhiyuan Wang,Xu Li,Shikang Guo,Wei Meng,Quan Liu,Jie Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ForeSightGuide是一个面向视障人士的预测性辅助导航框架，利用视觉语言模型(VLM)的推理能力来预测障碍物运动，过滤非威胁性物体，减少误报和认知过载。作者还引入了动态真实交通场景数据集，实验表明该方法在减少冗余告警(0.299/次)和保持低漏报率(0.112)方面达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的电子出行辅助工具虽然依赖VLM能提供丰富的环境理解，但在动态场景中会产生大量误报，导致认知过载。传统反应式系统无法区分真正威胁与非威胁性物体，需要一个能预测障碍物运动、前瞻性评估危险的辅助框架。

Method: 提出ForeSightGuide框架，核心是将语义场景理解与预测性危险评估相结合。不同于反应式系统，它利用VLM的推理能力预判障碍物的运动轨迹，过滤不构成威胁的物体，输出简洁、可执行的导航指引。同时构建了一个复杂动态真实交通场景的新数据集用于评测预测能力。

Result: 在公开基准和自建数据集上的大量实验表明，ForeSightGuide达到SOTA性能。具体而言，将冗余告警减少到每个引导输出0.299次，同时保持低至0.112的漏报率，有效缓解信息过载并保证安全行走辅助的有效性。

Conclusion: ForeSightGuide验证了利用VLM推理能力进行预测性危险评估的可行性，为视障人士出行辅助提供了有效解决方案，在减少认知负担和保证安全性之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Electronic travel aids are pivotal for the independent mobility of the visually impaired. While Vision-Language Models (VLMs) offer rich environmental understanding, they often suffer from excessive false positives in dynamic scenarios, leading to cognitive overload. To address this, we present ForeSightGuide, an anticipatory assistive guidance framework that couples semantic scene understanding with predictive hazard assessment. Unlike reactive systems, ForeSightGuide leverages the reasoning capabilities of VLMs to anticipate obstacle motion, effectively filtering out non-threatening objects to provide concise, actionable guidance. To validate our approach, we introduce a novel dataset captured in complex, dynamic real-world traffic scenes, designed to benchmark predictive capabilities. Extensive experiments on both public benchmarks and our proposed dataset demonstrate that ForeSightGuide achieves state-of-the-art performance. Notably, it significantly mitigates information overload by reducing redundant alerts to 0.299 per guidance output while maintaining a low missed-hazard rate of 0.112, proving its efficacy for safe walking assistance.

</details>


### [70] [GrabVG: Graph-Attentive Binding for Visual Grounding in UAV Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18996)
*Chaowei Wang,Yan Di,Jingjun Sun,Baozhe Liu,Jiaxu Tian,Yuheng Li,Guangqian Guo,Shan Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GrabVG是一个受人类视觉搜索启发的无人机航拍视觉定位框架，将接地区分解为预注意假设搜索和图注意特征绑定两个阶段，通过蒸馏引导的候选提议生成、文本感知过滤和稀疏图上的跨实例拓扑关系传播，在AerialVG和AerialSense上分别达到67.31%和80.34%的Acc@0.5精度，显著超过基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机航拍图像中目标物体小、密集分布、视觉相似性高，造成高视觉冗余和强拓扑歧义，现有视觉-语言对齐或密集上下文交互方法难以区分细微的实例间差异并有效利用空间拓扑结构，导致拥挤场景下定位不准确。

Method: 将视觉定位显式分解为两个阶段：(1)预注意假设搜索——通过蒸馏引导的候选提议生成和文本感知假设过滤，生成紧凑可靠的物体假设集，减少背景干扰和语义不匹配；(2)图注意特征绑定——将假设组织为稀疏图，通过图注意力机制联合绑定和传播语言引导的实例内视觉线索与实例间拓扑关系，实现高效空间推理和精确目标定位。

Result: 在AerialVG和AerialSense基准上分别达到67.31%和80.34%的Acc@0.5，比对应基线分别高出10.55和8.76个百分点，实现了良好的精度-速度权衡。

Conclusion: 通过模拟人类视觉搜索的预注意-注意两阶段机制，并利用图结构编码实例间拓扑关系，能够有效解决无人机航拍场景中小目标密集分布带来的定位挑战，验证了受认知启发的架构在视觉定位任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Visual grounding in Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) imagery aims to localize a target object in complex bird's-eye-view scenes according to a natural language description. However, the abundance of small, densely distributed, and visually similar objects creates high visual redundancy, while repetitive local configurations give rise to strong topological ambiguity. Existing approaches mainly focus on visual--language feature alignment or dense contextual interaction, yet they struggle to distinguish subtle inter-instance differences and effectively exploit spatial topological structures, leading to inaccurate grounding in highly crowded scenarios. To address these challenges, we propose $\textbf{GrabVG}$, a novel visual grounding framework inspired by human visual search. GrabVG explicitly decomposes grounding into two sequential stages: $\textit{preattentive hypothesis search}$ and $\textit{graph-attentive feature binding}$. Specifically, we first generate a compact set of reliable object hypotheses through distillation-guided proposal induction and text-aware hypothesis filtering, substantially reducing background distractions and semantic mismatches. These hypotheses are then organized into a sparse graph, where language-guided intra-instance visual cues and inter-instance topological relationships are jointly bound and propagated via graph attention, enabling efficient spatial reasoning and accurate target localization. Extensive experiments on AerialVG and AerialSense show that GrabVG achieves a favorable accuracy--speed trade-off, reaching 67.31$\%$ and 80.34$\%$ Acc@0.5 and outperforming the corresponding baselines by 10.55 and 8.76 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [71] [Mise-en-Scène: Implicit Layout Emergence in Diffusion Transformers for Human-AI Design Co-Creation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19000)
*Zipeng Xu,Ryan Murdock,Umberto Michieli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Mise-en-Scène两阶段框架，利用预训练图像编辑扩散Transformer隐式生成图文布局，再通过确定性匹配-放置步骤将原始高分辨率素材放置到草稿位置，在PrismLayersPlus基准上取得了最优感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图形设计自动合成方法将布局预测（显式边界框坐标）与视觉合成分离，导致构图僵硬、素材缩放不当。作者探讨是否可以让布局在预训练图像编辑扩散Transformer内部隐式涌现，从而实现布局与渲染画布的联合生成。

Method: 两阶段框架：(1) 第一阶段，用经过knockout选择的LoRA最小化适配预训练扩散Transformer，联合生成元素排列与渲染画布的完整设计草稿；(2) 第二阶段，确定性匹配-放置步骤，将原始高分辨率素材移动到草稿位置，确保素材保真度，生成可编辑的分层设计而非扁平图像。

Result: 在PrismLayersPlus基准上，Mise-en-Scène生成的设计在感知质量上最接近ground truth，大幅超过LLM布局规划器和专用布局Transformer；匹配-放置阶段进一步弥合了与ground-truth合成图像的保真度差距。

Conclusion: 最小化适配预训练Transformer足以实现高质量的多元素设计合成，无需专门的条件机制；隐式布局涌现结合确定性素材放置，兼顾了构图质量与素材保真度。

Abstract: Automating graphic design synthesis from user-provided elements requires both a coherent overall composition and the exact preservation of each asset. Existing methods predict a layout as explicit bounding-box coordinates with a language model and then paste the assets into it, which separates spatial planning from visual synthesis and tends to produce rigid, mis-scaled compositions. We instead ask whether the layout can emerge implicitly inside a pretrained image-editing diffusion transformer. We present Mise-en-Scène, a two-stage framework. In the first stage, a diffusion transformer adapted with a small, knockout-selected LoRA drafts a complete design in which the arrangement of the elements emerges jointly with the rendered canvas. In the second stage, a deterministic match-and-place step moves the original high-resolution assets to the drafted positions, which guarantees exact asset fidelity and yields an editable, layered design that a designer can keep refining rather than a flat image. Notably, a minimal adaptation of the pretrained transformer already suffices, without the specialized conditioning machinery commonly introduced for multi-element generation. On the large-scale PrismLayersPlus benchmark, the designs produced by Mise-en-Scène are the closest to the ground truth in perceived quality among all compared methods, by a wide margin over both an LLM layout planner and a specialized layout transformer, while our match-and-place stage bridges the remaining fidelity gap to the ground-truth composites.

</details>


### [72] [One-Stage Object Detectors in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19014)
*Jonel Roman,Ryan Sirjue,Peter Nguyen,Daniel Krutky,Juan Jesus,Sudip Dhakal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文是面向自动驾驶的一阶段目标检测器综述，系统回顾了YOLOv1、SSD、RetinaNet、EfficientDet、FCOS、CenterNet及YOLOv10等架构的设计选择、特征融合策略、损失函数、部署权衡与基准性能，并总结了自动驾驶常用数据集、评估指标、开放挑战和未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶依赖快速可靠的感知系统实时检测道路目标，一阶段目标检测器在速度与精度之间提供了关键平衡。作者旨在通过综述而非新方法实现，为研究人员梳理一阶段检测器的演进脉络、设计权衡及基准与实际部署之间的差距。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法，按时间线梳理一阶段检测器演进（含锚框检测器与anchor-free检测器），对比各模型在架构设计、特征融合、损失函数、部署权衡和基准性能等方面的差异，并归纳数据集、指标、挑战与未来研究方向。

Result: 综述揭示了一阶段检测器在速度、精度、效率与鲁棒性之间的权衡规律，强调了基准结果与真实自动驾驶部署之间仍存在显著差距，并指出了开放挑战（如小目标、恶劣天气、长尾场景）及未来研究方向。

Conclusion: 一阶段目标检测器已在自动驾驶感知中扮演核心角色，但基准性能不代表可靠的现实世界表现，仍需在鲁棒性、泛化能力与高效部署上持续探索。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles depend on fast and reliable perception systems to detect surrounding vehicles, pedestrians, cyclists, traffic signs, and other road objects in real time. This paper presents a comprehensive survey and analysis of one-stage object detectors for autonomous driving rather than an implementation of a new detection system. The survey reviews the evolution of major one-stage detectors, including YOLOv1, SSD, RetinaNet, EfficientDet, anchor-free detectors such as FCOS and CenterNet, and recent real-time models such as YOLOv10. It compares these architectures through their design choices, feature-fusion strategies, loss functions, deployment trade-offs, and reported benchmark performance. The paper also summarizes commonly used autonomous-driving datasets, evaluation metrics, open challenges, and future research directions. Overall, this survey highlights how one-stage detectors balance speed, accuracy, efficiency, and robustness, while also emphasizing the remaining gap between benchmark results and dependable real-world autonomous-driving performance.

</details>


### [73] [Orthogonal Polynomial Approximation for Matrix Log Normalization in Global Covariance Pooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19021)
*Md Rifat Ur Rahman,Md Raihan Khan,Md Sakib Hossain Shovon,Pietro Liò,Mohammad Ali Moni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需特征分解的矩阵对数（matrix logarithm）归一化方法用于全局协方差池化（GCP）。通过均值特征值预归一化将谱中心移到1附近，再用度数8的Chebyshev多项式展开和三项矩阵递推近似log(A)，前向和后向传播全部变成GEMM操作，梯度保持有界，避免了经典的1/(λ_i-λ_j)不稳定项。在三个细粒度基准和ImageNet-1k上，该分解无关对数比谱对数和平方根近似更快更准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GCP对细粒度识别有效，但协方差矩阵位于SPD流形上需在欧氏分类器前归一化。理论上最优的矩阵对数（MLN-COV）因其基于特征分解的梯度数值不稳定而被弃用，作者想证明这种不稳定性源自分部计算方式而非对数本身，从而恢复流形忠实映射的优势。

Method: 1) 均值特征值预归一化（mean-eigenvalue pre-normalization）将谱中心移近1，远离log的奇异点，并用标量后补偿项在闭式形式下恢复log(A)的奇异部分。2) 用度数8的Chebyshev多项式展开通过三项矩阵递推近似矩阵对数，前向和后向全部为GEMM操作，梯度在谱支撑内有界，不产生1/(λ_i-λ_j)不稳定项。3) 使用匹配的反向递推计算梯度，并将Legendre、Laguerre、Taylor和Padé展开作为对照以隔离基函数和目标函数的作用。

Result: 在三个细粒度基准和ImageNet-1k上，无特征分解的矩阵对数归一化比谱对数和平方根近似更快且更准确。在匹配基与度数条件下，log目标优于平方根目标，证明性能提升来自忠于Riemannian映射而非更好的多项式族。

Conclusion: 矩阵对数的数值不稳定性不是其对数的固有缺陷，而是谱分解计算的伪影。通过多项式近似和谱重标定可以稳定高效地恢复流形忠实的GCP归一化，既优于平方根近似也优于谱对数。

Abstract: Global Covariance Pooling (GCP) improves deep networks by capturing second-order feature statistics, and is especially effective for fine-grained recognition. Because covariance matrices live on the Symmetric Positive Definite (SPD) manifold, a normalization step is required before the Euclidean classifier. The faithful choice is the matrix logarithm (MLN-COV), which maps the SPD manifold to its tangent space; in practice it was abandoned in favour of the matrix square root because its eigendecomposition-based gradient is numerically unstable. We show that this instability is an artifact of computing the logarithm spectrally, not of the logarithm itself. Approximating the logarithm with finite polynomials in the covariance matrix removes the eigendecomposition from both passes: every operation becomes a General Matrix Multiplication (GEMM), the gradient stays bounded on the spectral support of the pre-normalized covariance, and the unstable 1/(lambda_i-lambda_j) term never appears. The key ingredient is a mean-eigenvalue pre-normalization that centres the spectrum near 1, away from the singularity of log, with a scalar post-compensation that returns the singular part of log(A) in closed form. Our recommended normalizer is a degree-8 Chebyshev expansion evaluated by a three-term matrix recurrence, with a matching reverse recurrence for the backward pass; Legendre, Laguerre, Taylor and Pade expansions are studied as controls that isolate the roles of the basis and of the target function. On three fine-grained benchmarks and ImageNet-1k the decomposition-free logarithm is both faster and more accurate than the spectral logarithm and than the square-root approximations it replaces, and at matched basis and degree the log target beats the square-root target, confirming that the gain comes from the faithful Riemannian map rather than from a better polynomial family.

</details>


### [74] [Counterfactual Contrastive Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19032)
*Yunlong He,Pietro Gori*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于对比分析(CA)的、无需分类器的视觉反事实解释(VCE)生成方法。它直接以数据分布为操作对象，利用StyleGAN2的F空间而非W空间来保持细节，并扩展了传统CA框架以支持每个数据集中存在多个显著因子。在三个医学影像数据集上验证了优于现有方法的反事实生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VCE方法依赖于分类器本身，因此容易受到分类器偏差（如捷径特征、校准误差）的影响。作者希望摆脱对分类器的依赖，提供模型无关的反事实解释，从而避免分类器失败模式带来的误导性解释。

Method: 1) 基于对比分析框架，将两个类别数据集（如健康与患病）的共同生成因子与各自显著因子解耦；2) 仅交换显著因子以生成反事实图像；3) 使用StyleGAN2的F空间替代传统的W空间以提升细节保真；4) 针对VCE任务改进传统CA损失函数，允许每个数据集中存在多个显著因子。

Result: 在三个医学影像数据集上，该方法相比现有VCE方法展现了更优的反事实生成质量，且因不依赖分类器而对分类器偏差更鲁棒。

Conclusion: 通过直接从数据分布层面操作而非分类决策边界，实现了模型无关、对分类器偏差不敏感的视觉反事实解释，为医学影像等领域的可解释AI提供了新思路。

Abstract: Visual Counterfactual Explanations (VCEs) aim to explain image classifiers by generating minimally edited and realistic versions of an input image that change the classifier's prediction. Existing VCE methods are inherently classifier-dependent and therefore susceptible to classifier biases and failure modes, such as sensitivity to shortcut features and calibration errors. In this paper, we propose a classifier-free approach for visual counterfactual generation based on Contrastive Analysis (CA). Given two datasets corresponding to different classes (e.g., healthy and patients), we disentangle the generative factors that are common across the two datasets from those that are salient to each dataset, and generate counterfactual images by swapping only the salient factors. By operating directly on data distributions rather than decision boundaries, our method provides model-agnostic VCEs that are less sensitive to classifier biases. Our approach leverages the high-quality synthesis and well-structured latent space of StyleGAN2. We use the feature space F, instead than the usual W-space, to improve detail preservation. Unlike conventional CA approaches, which typically assume salient factors in only one dataset, we introduce an adapted framework and loss functions for VCE that allow multiple salient factors in each dataset. We evaluate our method on three medical imaging datasets and demonstrate superior counterfactual generation quality compared to existing approaches.

</details>


### [75] [USR-Drive: Unified Driving Scene Representation via Joint Denoising of 3D Gaussians and Boxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19036)
*Li-Heng Chen,Haokai Pang,Chengye Su,Jiarun Liu,Qifeng Chen,Ziqian Ni,Jianxin Huang,Shi-Sheng Huang,Hongbo Fu,Sheng Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: USR-Drive提出统一条件生成框架，在共享场景表征中联合恢复动态稠密几何（3D高斯）和实例级物体布局（3D框），通过多模态扩散Transformer和统一位置编码实现两种模态的互约束与增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将动态重建与实例级感知视为分离任务，导致动态重建欠约束、3D检测缺乏几何基础。两种任务本质共享同一3D世界状态估计目标，应统一建模以互相增强。

Method: 将稠密高斯基元与稀疏3D框表示为两个对齐的潜变量token流，用统一多模态扩散Transformer联合去噪；提出Unified Positional Encoding（UPE）在共享度量时空坐标系中对齐异构token，使两类模态作为互约束的状态变量而非外部条件或解耦模块。

Result: 在nuScenes和VKitti数据集上，动态重建和3D检测均取得SOTA结果。几何为框预测提供稠密度量证据，框为几何表示提供实例级结构先验以维持空间一致性并减少歧义。

Conclusion: 统一条件生成框架验证了动态重建与实例级感知可被联合建模并互相增强，为自动驾驶场景理解提供了统一表征范式。

Abstract: Spatial representation learning for autonomous driving aims to map raw visual signals into structured 3D scene representations, where object-centric bounding boxes and rendering-oriented 3D primitives (\eg, 3D Gaussians) serve as two distinct yet highly complementary levels for scene understanding. Existing methods typically treat dynamic reconstruction and instance-level perception as separate tasks, despite their shared goal of estimating the underlying 3D world state. As a result, dynamic reconstruction is under-constrained while 3D detection lacks geometric grounding. To address this gap, we propose USR-Drive, a unified conditional generative framework that, given only posed multi-view driving videos, jointly recovers dense dynamic geometry and instance-level object layouts within a shared scene representation. Specifically, USR-Drive represents dense Gaussian primitives and sparse 3D bounding boxes as two aligned latent token streams and jointly denoises them with a unified multi-modal diffusion Transformer. Unlike prior paradigms that use boxes as external conditions or predict them with detached modules, USR-Drive treats them as mutually constrained state variables with a Unified Positional Encoding (UPE) that aligns heterogeneous tokens within a shared metric spatiotemporal coordinate. Via such unified representation and generative framework, the two modalities reinforce each other: geometry supplies dense metric evidence for box prediction, while boxes provide instance-level structural priors that help preserve spatial consistency and reduce ambiguity in sequential 3D geometric representation. Our approach successfully delivers state-of-the-art results for both dynamic reconstruction and 3D detection on the nuScenes and VKitti datasets.

</details>


### [76] [Generalized Audio-Driven Synthesis of Precise Drummer Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19055)
*Álvaro G. Iñesta,Mattia Ryffel,Amit H. Bermano,Robert W. Sumner,Martin Guay*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个基于扩散模型的音乐驱动击鼓动画生成框架，通过双目标损失函数解耦骨骼完整性与鼓棒精度，实现厘米级击鼓精度；同时提出两个新评估指标（impact-to-target distance和audio-motion correlation score），并借助自建数据集和数据增强策略实现野外音频的泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音乐驱动的角色动画在娱乐和交互式教育中有广泛应用，但合成逼真的击鼓动作极具挑战性：击鼓涉及高加速度动态与极端时空精度的固有矛盾。现有方法多依赖动作匹配或MIDI输入，难以泛化到多样化的真实世界音频，且缺乏能够区分精确击鼓与噪声运动的标准评估指标。

Method: 提出一个生成式扩散框架，采用双目标损失函数：将骨骼完整性损失与鼓棒精度损失解耦，实现厘米级鼓棒精度同时保持自然身体动态。使用自建数据集配合数据增强策略提升对野外音频的泛化能力。提出两个新评估指标：impact-to-target distance量化空间精度，audio-motion correlation score评估时间对齐。

Result: 量化分析和用户研究表明，系统生成的高质量运动在视觉上往往与真实录制表演难以区分。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了音乐驱动击鼓动画中高加速度动态与精度需求的矛盾，实现了从真实音频到精确击鼓动作的生成，并通过新评估指标和完善的泛化策略验证了系统性能。

Abstract: Music-driven character animation enables and enhances transformative applications in entertainment and interactive education. However, synthesizing realistic drumming motion from audio remains challenging due to the inherent tension between high-acceleration dynamics and the need for extreme spatial-temporal precision. Existing approaches, often reliant on motion matching or MIDI input, struggle with generalizing to diverse real-world audio. Moreover, the field lacks standardized evaluation metrics capable of distinguishing precise drumming from noisy motion. In this paper, we introduce a generative diffusion framework featuring a dual-objective loss function that decouples skeletal integrity from drumstick precision, thus enabling centimeter-level stick precision without sacrificing natural body dynamics. Additionally, leveraging our own dataset and data augmentation strategy, the model generalizes to non-curated, in-the-wild audio. To rigorously evaluate performance, we propose two novel metrics: an impact-to-target distance to quantify spatial precision and an audio-motion correlation score to assess temporal alignment. Our quantitative analysis and user studies demonstrate that our system generates high-quality motion that is often indistinguishable from ground-truth performances.

</details>


### [77] [When Two Tracers Disagree: An Investigation of Multimodal Fusion for Clinical PET/CT Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19063)
*Jack A. Johnson,Bartłomiej W. Papież*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文在公开DEEP-PSMA Challenge数据集上系统评估了多模态（PSMA和FDG PET/CT）图像融合策略用于全身PET/CT病变分割，比较了早期融合（单编码器单/双解码器）与中间融合（双编码器交叉注意力U-Net），结果表明单示踪剂模型仍是更强基线，融合策略未持续超越单示踪剂模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PSMA和FDG PET/CT在前列腺癌中提供互补的生物学信息，联合两者可能捕获单独一种示踪剂会遗漏的异质性肿瘤表型。但目前缺乏多模态融合的有效深度学习架构共识，亟需系统评估不同融合策略的性能。

Method: 使用公开DEEP-PSMA Challenge数据集，训练示踪剂特异的3D nnU-Net基线，并比较三种融合策略：(i) 早期融合-单编码器单解码器(OEOD)；(ii) 早期融合-单编码器双解码器(OETD)；(iii) 中间融合-双编码器交叉注意力U-Net(DECA-UNet)。

Result: 单示踪剂基线表现强（PSMA Dice=0.93，FDG Dice=0.81）。融合结果参差：OEOD组合Dice为0.90（在更简单的非示踪剂特异任务上），OETD达到PSMA/FDG=0.69/0.64，DECA-UNet达到PSMA/FDG=0.76/0.57。融合虽在PSMA分割上表现合理，但FDG性能下降，无策略持续超越单示踪剂基线。

Conclusion: 在评估条件下，示踪剂特异模型仍是更强基线；多模态融合要产生临床有效增益，需要能更好保留示踪剂特异表征的架构设计。代码已开源。

Abstract: PSMA and FDG PET/CT visualise complementary biological information in prostate cancer. Combining both tracers could capture heterogeneous tumour phenotypes that may be missed by either alone, yet there is no consensus on effective deep learning architectures for fusing these modalities. We evaluated multimodal image-fusion strategies for automatic whole-body PET/CT lesion segmentation to estimate total tumour burden. Using the public DEEP-PSMA Challenge dataset, we trained tracer-specific 3D nnU-Net baselines and compared (i) early fusion with a single encoder and one decoder (OEOD) or two decoders (OETD), and (ii) intermediate fusion via a dual-encoder cross-attention U-Net (DECA-UNet). Tracer-specific baselines performed strongly (PSMA Dice = 0.93; FDG = 0.81). Fusion yielded mixed results: OEOD produced a combined Dice of 0.90 (on an easier, non-tracer-specific task), whilst the tracer-specific fusion models reached PSMA/FDG = 0.69/0.64 (OETD) and 0.76/0.57 (DECA-UNet). Whilst fusion often provided reasonable PSMA segmentation, FDG performance degraded and no strategy consistently exceeded the single-tracer baselines. Under the evaluated setting, tracer-specific models remain the stronger baseline; clinically useful gains from multimodal fusion will likely require architectures that better preserve tracer specific representations. Our code is available at: https://github.com/JackJ3636/DEEP_PSMA_code

</details>


### [78] [GS-VLA: Plug-and-Play Viewpoint Canonicalization for Frozen VLA Policies via Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19066)
*Yechan Park,HyunJin Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GS-VLA，一个仅4M参数的3D高斯规范化器（canonicalizer），前置在冻结的VLA策略前，通过将视角偏移重构为局部新视角合成问题，在不重训练策略的前提下大幅恢复因相机视角变化导致的性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略隐式假设训练和部署相机配置一致，但实际中相机安装位置的微小位移就会导致性能从约90%暴跌至约10%。现有方案（大规模微调、生成式数据增强）计算昂贵且有灾难性遗忘风险。

Method: 将视角偏移格式化为局部新视角合成问题，在Locality假设（相机扰动相对于工作空间保持在小有界区域内）下，将视角归一化简化为场景无关、策略无关的去遮挡（disocclusion）任务。用4M参数的3D高斯规范化器实现，前置到冻结的VLA策略前，无需修改策略权重。

Result: GS-VLA在三个正交维度上提升性能：策略架构、未见任务套件、扰动尺度；能恢复视角偏移下大部分丢失的性能，且无需策略重训练。

Conclusion: 轻量级视觉模块可以在不重训练策略的前提下恢复视角偏移造成的大部分性能损失，这是首个直接利用3D高斯新视角合成进行VLA观测空间自适应的工作。

Abstract: This paper proposes a lightweight, plug-and-play framework that improves robustness to viewpoint shifts in Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies without policy retraining. To our knowledge, this is the first approach to directly leverage 3D Gaussian-based novel-view synthesis for observation-space adaptation in VLA policies. Current VLA performance relies on the implicit assumption that training and deployment camera configurations are identical. Our experiments show that even a small displacement of the camera mount can reduce the success rate on the LIBERO benchmark from about 90% to about 10% in the worst case. Prior approaches, such as large-scale fine-tuning or generative data augmentation, are computationally expensive and risk catastrophic forgetting. To address this, viewpoint shifts are reformulated as a localized novel-view synthesis problem. Under a Locality assumption, that camera perturbations remain within a small bounded region relative to the workspace, viewpoint normalization reduces to a scene- and policy-independent disocclusion task. Our work implements this idea with a 4M-parameter 3D-Gaussian canonicalizer prepended to a frozen VLA policy. Without modifying policy weights, GS-VLA improves performance across three orthogonal axes: (1) Policy architectures, (2) Unseen task suites, and (3) Perturbation scales. These results show that a lightweight visual module can recover a large fraction of the performance lost under viewpoint shift, without policy retraining.

</details>


### [79] [ReWEIGH the Evidence: Calibrating Token-Level Ordinal Visual Evidence to Mitigate Hallucinations in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19075)
*Jihae Jeong,Junha Choi,Hwanjo Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReWEIGH是一种无需训练的推理时解码干预方法，通过聚合视觉标记的词汇排名证据并与从无标注图像估计的标记特定参考值比较，来减少大视觉语言模型（LVLM）中的幻觉对象提及。在四个7B主干上幻觉减少最高达21.3%，平均额外延迟仅1.33%/token，且泛化至六种架构家族、32B参数规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM在解码时经常产生输入图像不支持的幻觉内容。现有方法缺乏一种候选特定的、度量图像对当前token支持强度的办法。视觉token状态通过输出头投影可以揭示词汇偏好，但位置间的概率幅度不可直接比较，词汇排名虽提供尺度不变的池化基础，但不同token的典型排名证据存在系统性差异，需要一种token特定的校准方法。

Method: ReWEIGH包含三个核心组件：(1) 利用输出头投影视觉token状态，获取每个视觉位置的词汇排名（rank）作为尺度不变的证据；(2) 在prefill阶段缓存图像证据，聚合各视觉位置的排名；(3) 从无标注图像估计每个候选token的token特定参考值（reference），推理时仅对低于参考值的候选施加有界的（bounded）惩罚，从而实现训练无关的解码干预。

Result: 在四个7B主干上，ReWEIGH将幻觉对象提及减少最高21.3%，同时基本保持或提升描述性与通用性能。缓存证据后平均每token额外延迟仅1.33%，且减少效果扩展到六个架构家族、最高32B参数规模。

Conclusion: ReWEIGH证明了一种无需训练的、基于视觉token证据聚合与token特定参考校准的解码干预方法，可以有效且高效地抑制LVLM幻觉，同时保持通用性能，具有跨架构泛化能力。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) often hallucinate, generating content that the input image does not support. Preventing such content during decoding calls for a candidate-specific measure of how strongly the image supports the token under consideration. The model's visual-token states offer a natural source of this evidence because projecting each state through the output head reveals which vocabulary items that position favors. These position-wise readouts cannot be pooled directly because their probability magnitudes are not comparable across visual positions. Vocabulary ranks provide a scale-invariant basis for pooling, but tokens still differ systematically in their typical rank-based evidence. We propose ReWEIGH, a training-free decoding intervention that aggregates these ranks across visual positions and compares each candidate with a token-specific reference estimated from unlabeled images. At inference, ReWEIGH caches the image evidence during prefill and applies a bounded penalty only to candidates that fall below their reference. On four 7B backbones, ReWEIGH reduces hallucinated object mentions by up to 21.3% while largely preserving or improving descriptive and general performance. With evidence cached, the average added latency is 1.33% per token, and the reductions extend across six architecture families to 32B parameters.

</details>


### [80] [Subgroup performance analysis of adaptation strategies for chest X-ray foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19078)
*Dhruv Gupta,Emma A. M. Stanley,Fabio De Sousa Ribeiro,Sujal Desai,Ben Glocker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了将冻结的Rad-DINO胸部X光编码器用于下游医学影像任务时，三种参数高效微调策略（线性头、MLP、注意力池化）对八个病理在种族、性别、成像视角亚组上的公平性影响。结果表明注意力池化在判别性能上最优且对受保护属性的编码最强，但更强的性能与属性编码并不对应更小的亚组差异，公平性影响是任务依赖且不可预测的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型越来越多地被用于下游医学影像任务，但微调策略的选择如何影响亚组公平性尚未被充分理解。理解这一点对于确保医学AI在不同人群中的公平表现至关重要。

Method: 使用MIMIC-CXR数据集，在保持流行率、人口统计学平衡的测试集上，对冻结的Rad-DINO胸部X光编码器应用三种参数高效适配技术（线性头、MLP、注意力池化模块），评估八种病理在种族、性别、成像视角亚组上的分类性能和子组差异，并探测每个适配器对受保护属性的编码强度。

Result: 注意力池化在整体判别性能上最强，且对属性（尤其是种族）的编码最强，但更好的整体性能并未一致地减少亚组差异。更强的属性编码不代表更大的差异：早期网络层对种族编码最弱但产生了最大的亚组性能差距。探索不同的注意力池化层组合也未发现层组合、属性编码强度与公平性之间的一致关系。

Conclusion: 更丰富、更具表达力的表征可以提升准确性，但公平性影响是任务依赖且不可预测的，必须针对每个任务直接评估，而非仅从编码强度或整体性能推断。

Abstract: Foundation models are increasingly adapted for downstream medical imaging tasks, yet the influence of the chosen adaptation strategy on subgroup fairness remains poorly understood. We investigate how three parameter-efficient adaptation techniques, including linear heads on the raw CLS token, an MLP, and an attention-pooling module over multi-layer patch features, affect both pathology classification performance and subgroup disparities when applied to the frozen Rad-DINO chest X-ray encoder. Using MIMIC-CXR, we evaluate eight pathologies across race, sex, and imaging-view subgroups on a prevalence-preserving, demographically balanced test set, and additionally probe how strongly each adapter encodes protected attributes. We find that attention pooling achieves the strongest overall discriminative performance and encodes attributes, particularly race, most strongly, but that improved overall performance does not consistently reduce subgroup disparities. Notably, stronger attribute encoding did not correspond to larger disparities: early network layers encoded race most weakly yet produced the largest subgroup performance gaps. Exploring different attention-pooling layer combinations further revealed no consistent relationship between the layers pooled, attribute encoding strength, and subgroup fairness. Our results indicate that richer, more expressive representations can improve accuracy while leaving fairness implications task-dependent and unpredictable, which must be assessed directly and per-task rather than inferred from encoding strength or overall performance alone.

</details>


### [81] [SPK: Eliciting Structured Prior Knowledge for Interpretable Out-of-Distribution Detection in Real-Time Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19080)
*Changshun Wu,Weicheng He,Xiaowei Huang,Saddek Bensalem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SPK（Structured Prior Knowledge）框架，从预训练目标检测器中显式挖掘与OoD（域外）幻觉相关的潜在先验知识，构建紧凑的五维结构化表示用于OoD检测，在多种检测器架构和多个OoD基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标检测器常对训练类别之外的物体产生过度自信的预测（OoD幻觉）。现有方法要么直接在检测器表示上构建评分函数，要么修改检测器本身来抑制幻觉，但检测器表示中隐含的潜在先验从未被显式解码用于OoD检测。

Method: SPK利用分布内数据和幻觉诱导样本作为诊断监督，从预训练检测器中激发部件级语义概念（part-level semantic concepts），而非仅用于拒绝或检测器适配。将这些语义先验与几何先验和上下文先验整合，形成紧凑的五维SPK表示，用于OoD检测。

Result: 在多种目标检测器架构和多个OoD基准上，SPK达到最先进的OoD检测性能。实验发现预训练检测器编码了远超常规利用的丰富潜在知识，且这些知识可以被显式激发并组织为紧凑、结构化、可解释的知识空间。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了预训练检测器中潜藏的可解释先验知识可以显式提取用于可靠性分析，为提升目标检测器可靠性提供了一条主动式路线。代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: Object detectors often produce over-confident predictions for objects outside their training categories, leading to so-called out-of-distribution (OoD) hallucinations. Existing approaches for detecting or mitigating such hallucinations typically either construct scoring functions directly over learned object detector representations or modify the object detector itself to suppress hallucination emergence. However, the latent priors implicitly encoded in these representations remain largely unexplored and have not been explicitly decoded for OoD detection. To uncover and exploit these latent priors, we propose Structured Prior Knowledge (SPK), a hallucination-oriented framework that explicitly elicits OoD-relevant priors from pretrained object detectors. Specifically, SPK leverages in-distribution data and hallucination-inducing samples as diagnostic supervision to elicit part-level semantic concepts underlying object detector decision-making, rather than using them merely for rejection or object detector adaptation. The elicited semantic priors are further integrated with geometric and contextual priors to form a compact five-dimensional SPK representation for OoD detection. Extensive experiments across diverse object detector architectures and multiple OoD benchmarks demonstrate that SPK achieves state-of-the-art OoD detection. Our findings reveal that pretrained object detectors already encode substantially richer latent knowledge than is typically exploited for OoD detection. More importantly, this knowledge can be explicitly elicited and organized into a compact, structured, and interpretable knowledge space for prediction reliability analysis. This suggests a promising proactive route for improving object detector reliability by explicitly uncovering and leveraging latent priors. Code and data are available at: https://gricad-gitlab.univ-grenoble-alpes.fr/dnn-safety/spk

</details>


### [82] [Detecting Backdoors in Object Detection via Pre-NMS Prediction Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19088)
*Longtian Wang,Zhengyu Zhao,Chenhao Lin,Le Yang,Shiwei Wang,Yuhan Zhi,Xiaofei Xie,Chao Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DistScan提出一种基于预NMS类别分布偏移的背door检测框架，无需权重访问、触发器知识或额外训练，仅通过干净验证集上的中间类别预测聚合，即可在场景级攻击下大幅提升检测准确率（相对最优基线提升27.32个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有目标检测模型的背door检测方法要么依赖触发器反转，要么依赖特定架构假设，无法可靠泛化到场景级攻击（单一触发器同时导致场景中所有对象异常）。需要一种无需权重访问、无需触发器知识、无需额外训练的通用检测方案。

Method: 核心观察：背door注入系统地使模型的预NMS预测类别分布偏离其训练类别频率，即使在没有触发器的干净输入上也是如此。DistScan在干净验证集上聚合中间类别预测，若所得分布与训练类别频率显著偏离，则将模型判定为被植入背door。

Result: 在MS-COCO和PASCAL VOC上，跨两种架构和三种场景级攻击场景，DistScan显著优于现有方法，平均检测准确率较最优适用基线提升27.32个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过一个简单但此前未被利用的观察，DistScan实现了无需模型权重、无需触发器先验、无需额外训练的背door检测，且对场景级攻击具有强泛化能力，是该方向上的一项有效进展。

Abstract: Object detection models deployed in safety-critical applications remain vulnerable to backdoor attacks that cause targeted misbehaviors when a hidden trigger is present. Existing detection methods either rely on trigger inversion or exploit architecture-specific assumptions, and critically, representative existing methods fail to generalize reliably to scene-level attacks, where a single trigger induces anomalous behavior across all objects in the scene simultaneously. We present DistScan, a backdoor detection framework based on a simple but previously unexploited observation: backdoor injection systematically shifts a model's pre-NMS prediction class distribution away from its training class frequencies, even on clean inputs without any trigger present. DistScan aggregates intermediate class predictions over a clean validation set and flags a model as backdoored if the resulting distribution deviates significantly from the training class frequencies, requiring no model weight access, no trigger knowledge, and no additional training. Extensive experiments on MS-COCO and PASCAL VOC across two architectures and three scene-level attack scenarios demonstrate that DistScan substantially outperforms existing methods, improving average detection accuracy over the best-performing applicable baseline by 27.32 percentage points.

</details>


### [83] [Image-Guided Pavement Defect Recognition in GPR Data with novel 3D Deep Learning Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19177)
*Yuandong Pan,Linjun Lu,Mudan Wang,Florian Noichl,Fan Xue,Brian Sheil,Lavindra de Silva,André Borrmann,Ioannis Brilakis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种经济高效的数据制备流程，结合正射RGB影像与3D GPR扫描生成标注的3D GPR数据集，并设计了一个专用3D CNN架构（含残差连接、混合卷积核、深度与通道注意力）用于路面缺陷（修补与裂缝）二分类检测，在实际运营高速公路数据集上验证了其优于基线方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPR是土木与交通工程中广泛应用的 subsurface 无损检测技术，但其在大规模自动化检测中的实际应用受限于两大挑战：标注真实世界3D GPR数据的稀缺，以及缺乏针对3D GPR数据独特特性设计的深度学习模型。

Method: 1) 提出数据制备pipeline：利用对齐的路面RGB正射影像段与3D GPR扫描段，以路面表面可见缺陷为参考，将表面缺陷标签迁移到对应的GPR段，实现大规模高效标注。2) 提出专用3D CNN架构，包含残差连接、混合卷积核尺寸、depthwise与channelwise注意力机制，用于增强特征表示与缺陷分类。3) 在二分类任务（修补与裂缝检测）上进行评估。

Result: 实验表明所提出的网络在多个评估指标上优于基线架构；消融研究进一步确认了各设计组件的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文贡献了一个可扩展且实用的真实世界3D GPR数据集生成方法，以及一个新的深度学习框架，为GPR路面自动化检测提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Ground Penetrating Radar (GPR) is a widely adopted non-destructive sensing technology for subsurface inspection in civil and transportation engineering. Despite its potential for pavement condition assessment, the large-scale application of GPR in automated inspection has two key challenges: the scarcity of annotated real-world datasets and the lack of deep learning models designed for the unique characteristics of 3-Dimensional (3D) GPR data. This study addresses these limitations by firstly introducing a cost-effective data preparation pipeline that integrates orthomosaic Red Green Blue (RGB) imagery with 3D GPR scans to generate annotated 3D GPR datasets. The proposed method uses the aligned segments of RGB and GPR data, using pavement surface images as a reference to transfer labels of surface-visible defects to corresponding GPR segments, enabling efficient large-scale annotation in a real-world dataset collected on a highway section under operation. In addition to the dataset contribution, we propose a specialised 3D Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) architecture incorporating residual connections, mixed convolutional kernel sizes, and both depthwise and channelwise attention mechanisms to enhance feature representation and defect classification. The model is evaluated on binary classification tasks for detecting patch and crack defects in pavement structures. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed network outperforms baseline architectures across multiple evaluation metrics. Ablation studies further confirm the effectiveness of the designed architectural components. This work contributes a scalable and practical method for real-world dataset generation, along with a novel deep learning framework.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [84] [Scheduling and Routing with Degradation-Triggered Job Arrivals: An Application to Forest Firefighting with an Unmanned Aerial Vehicle Fleet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18140)
*Erdi Dasdemir,Esther Jose,Rajan Batta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个交织的调度与路由问题：新任务因现有任务状态恶化而出现，目标是在任务时间窗内最大化总剩余奖励。问题受"及时一针省九针"启发，应用于空中森林消防场景。作者建立了混合整数规划模型，并设计基于动态约束生成的混合模型以提升可扩展性，通过计算实验和案例研究验证性能，并开源代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的调度与路由问题常忽略"任务恶化会触发新任务"这一动态特性。在森林消防等场景中，延误干预会导致火势蔓延至相邻区域并生成新任务，现有模型难以捕捉这种交织的恶化-新增任务关系。作者希望建立能体现"及时干预的价值"的优化模型，填补这一研究空白。

Method: 将问题形式化为混合整数规划（MIP）模型，最大化保留总奖励。模型处理两个关键交互：任务需求在时间窗内随时间恶化，且恶化超过阈值时触发相邻区域产生新任务；位置默认奖励因未处理任务持续存在而下降。为提升可扩展性，提出基于动态约束生成的混合求解模型，避免一次性枚举所有约束，从而处理更大规模实例。

Result: 通过计算实验和森林消防案例研究评估了模型性能与实用性。动态约束生成混合模型相较于标准MIP在可扩展性上有显著提升，能够在更大规模实例上求解。案例研究验证了模型在实际森林消防场景中的可行性与应用价值。

Conclusion: 该交织调度路由问题模型有效捕捉了任务恶化引发新任务的动态，在森林消防场景中能最大化区域价值保留率，动态约束生成策略显著增强了大规模求解能力，且代码开源支持复现与后续研究。

Abstract: We define an intertwined scheduling and routing problem where new jobs appear due to the degradation of the existing jobs. Specifically, once a job arrives at a potential job location, a time window begins during which the demand of the job can be fulfilled. The demand degrades within the time window, and once it surpasses a particular threshold, it triggers the arrival of new jobs. Each job location inherently possesses an initial default reward, and the presence of an unprocessed job at a location gradually reduces this default value. The overall objective is to maximize the total remaining reward. The underlying motivation of this problem aligns with the proverb ``a stitch in time saves nine," and the problem itself carries practical implications. We focus on the problem in the context of aerial forest firefighting. Each ignited area has a designated action window; delaying intervention causes the fire to grow, diminishing the area's value and causing it to spread to adjacent areas. We develop a mixed-integer programming model that maximizes value retention in wildfire-threatened regions, and a hybrid model based on dynamic constraint generation to enhance the scalability of the model. We evaluate the performance and practicality of our models through computational experiments and a case study. Additionally, we ensure the study's reproducibility and encourage further research by providing open access to the codebase of our model.

</details>


### [85] [Trust as a Field: A Macroscopic Representation for Vehicular Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18178)
*Md Mahmudul Islam,Shaurya Agarwal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种时空信任场（spatio-temporal trust-field）框架，将微观的车载信任聚合成空间和时间上的连续表示，并研究了从稀疏路侧单元（RSU）测量中重建完整信任场的问题，对比了坐标基深度学习基线与场信息深度学习方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有车联网信任评估方法主要在单车层面运作，难以推理信任在道路区段上的演化，缺乏从微观车辆信任到宏观道路区域信任的连续建模框架。

Method: 提出时空信任场框架，在道路区段上形式化定义信任场；使用合成轨迹进行受控仿真实验；对比两种重建方法：坐标基深度学习基线与将信任作为车辆携带的隐变量并通过聚合机制强制执行测量一致性的场信息深度学习。

Result: 场信息学习方法更准确地恢复了轨迹对齐的低信任模式，并获得了更好的重建误差。

Conclusion: 信任场框架为车联网信任评估提供了空间-时间连续建模能力，且场信息深度学习在稀疏RSU测量下的信任场重建优于通用坐标基方法。

Abstract: Trust assessment is a fundamental component of cooperative and connected vehicle systems. However, existing approaches operate primarily at the level of individual vehicles, making it difficult to reason about trust evolution across road segments. In this paper, we propose a spatio-temporal trust-field framework that aggregates microscopic vehicle-level trust into a continuous representation over space and time. The trust field is formally defined on road segments. We conducted simulation-based experiments using synthetic trajectories generated under controlled conditions, enabling analysis of trust-field behavior in simple road scenarios. Beyond theoretical modeling, we study an implication of the trust-field concept: reconstructing the full trust field from sparse roadside-unit (RSU) measurements. We compare (i) a coordinate-based deep learning baseline that learns a generic trust field from sparse samples and (ii) a field-informed deep learning method that treats trust as a latent quantity carried by vehicles and enforces measurement consistency through the aggregation mechanism. The field-informed approach more accurately recovers trajectory-aligned low-trust patterns and yields improved reconstruction error.

</details>


### [86] [Revisiting the "Push-T" Robot Manipulation Task with Agentic Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18227)
*Shuangyu Xie,Kaiyuan Chen,Ken Goldberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文重新审视Push-T经典机器人操作基准，发现LLM编码智能体（Claude Code with Fable 5）通过编程循环创建无需演示数据的算法策略，无需任何人类演示即达到100%成功率，且步数比最优扩散策略少46%，并将任务从T推广到整个字母表（Push-A至Push-Z），同时生成了Franka和UR5机械臂的3D跨具身仿真代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索新兴智能体机器人（Agentic Robotics）范式中，LLM编码智能体是否能在无演示数据的情况下，通过自主编写算法和仿真实验交互解决经典机器人操作基准任务Push-T，并以此挑战传统基于示范的模仿学习方法。

Method: 使用Claude Code with Fable 5编码智能体，通过agentic编码循环：智能体在线上找到2D gym仿真环境，通过仿真实验学习推杆力学，迭代优化策略；随后通过自生成课程将任务推广到全字母表，并使用自生成的仿真代码在Franka和UR5机械臂的3D跨具身仿真（含视觉反馈）中验证。

Result: 编码智能体在Push-T任务上实现了100%成功率，比使用200个人类演示训练的最优扩散策略减少46%的步数；成功将任务扩展到Push-A到Push-Z全集；在两种真实机械臂（Franka、UR5）的3D跨具身仿真中生成并验证了策略代码。

Conclusion: 编码智能体能够在无演示数据、无监督信号的前提下，通过自主编写代码和利用仿真环境迭代优化，解决并超越传统模仿学习在Push-T上的表现，展示了Agentic Robotics在自主策略发现方面的潜力。但所有验证均在仿真中进行，尚缺乏真实机器人部署证据。

Abstract: Push-T is an iconic benchmark for learning manipulation policies from human demonstrations. The robot must use a single point of contact to push a T-shaped block into a target pose. In this short paper, we revisit the Push-T task in the context of emerging advances in Agentic Robotics where an LLM coding agent -- Claude Code with Fable 5 -- is prompted to create an algorithmic solution that does not require any demonstration data. We study how effective the agentic coding loop can solve the Push-T task, and compare the resulting code as policy with the visuomotor imitation learning policy. Results suggest that the agent found the 2D gym simulation online, and used sim experiments to learn push mechanics, iteratively optimizing to achieve 100% success rate using 46% fewer steps than the best diffusion policy trained with 200 human demonstrations. The coding agent also solve extensions from T to the full alphabet (Push-A to Push-Z) using a self generated curriculum and generated simulation code for the Franka and UR5 robot arms in 3D cross-embodiment simulations with visual feedback. Videos, policies and details will be posted online.

</details>


### [87] [GigaBrain-WBC-0.5: A Behavior World Model for Robust Whole-Body Control with Environment Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18234)
*Ziyang Cheng,Tianshu Tang,Jinxin Lan,Xinze Chen,Yuhan Gong,Zhichao Liu,Changzhong Wu,Yahao Mao,Zongyan Deng,Mingxuan Ma,Huasen Xi,Yilong Liu,Yutong Wu,Xiaofeng Wang,Yang Wang,Yun Ye,Guan Huang,Xiaojie Jin,Zheng Zhu,Jiwen Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GigaBrain-WBC-0.5，首个用于人形机器人全身控制的Behavior World Model（BWM）。它不是纯反应式跟踪器，而是训练因果Transformer联合预测下一动作、下一状态和下一潜在行为命令的分布，使执行动作的网络同时建模环境对可行性的影响。通过自动地形标注管线从重定向运动中恢复完整3D接触几何，并在部署时利用预测分布在线检测不合理命令并拉回已学行为。在四个测试场景中均优于三种大规模跟踪基线，地形交互成功率81.3%（为最强基线4.3倍）、不合理命令下83.1%、摔倒恢复99.3%（为最强基线16.8倍），并成功在Unitree G1真机上完成硬件实验，简单微调后迁移至Maker L01。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全身运动跟踪器只在平坦地面上有效：它们是在空场景中训练的，从未学习地形和物体接触如何重塑其动力学，且试图通过不断扩充参考动作语料来在任何命令下保持平衡，这在可行行为变得依赖环境时失效。需要一个能建模环境影响的统一策略。

Method: 训练一个因果Transformer作为Behavior World Model，联合预测：下一动作、下一状态、以及下一潜在行为命令的分布。即"执行动作的网络同时也建模环境如何塑造其下一步行为"。配套设计了自动地形标注管线，从重定向运动中恢复完整3D接触几何，使地形标注能达到现有动作数据集规模。部署时，利用预测的概率分布在线检测不合理命令并将之拉回已学习的可行行为，实现"尽力而为"的任务执行。

Result: 在四种测试场景中对三种大规模跟踪基线取得最高成功率：地形交互81.3%（最强基线4.3倍）、不合理命令下83.1%、摔倒恢复99.3%（最强基线16.8倍）。硬件实验显示在缺失支撑和扰动下仍能鲁棒交互；Unitree G1的checkpoint经简单微调后成功迁移到Maker L01机器人。

Conclusion: GigaBrain-WBC-0.5证明了世界模型式架构（联合预测动作+状态+命令分布）能统一命令跟踪、环境交互和鲁棒性恢复，并具备跨硬件的可迁移性，是一项有实际硬件验证的人形全身控制工作。

Abstract: Whole-body motion tracking policies turn a humanoid into a robust control interface: the teleoperator---or an upstream model---only supplies a coarse movement intent, while the low-level policy keeps the robot balanced and physically feasible. Existing trackers deliver this interface only on flat ground: trained in empty scenes, they never learn how contact with terrain and objects reshapes their dynamics, and they attempt to teach the policy to balance under any command by continually enlarging the reference-motion corpus, which stops working once feasible behaviors become environment-dependent. We present GigaBrain-WBC-0.5, the first Behavior World Model (BWM) for humanoid whole-body control. Rather than a purely reactive tracker, we train a causal Transformer to jointly predict its next action, next state, and the distribution over its next latent behavior command, so the network that acts also models how the environment shapes what it can do next. An automatic terrain-annotation pipeline recovers full 3D contact geometry from retargeted motion, enabling terrain annotation at the scale of existing motion datasets. The predicted distribution is reused at deployment to detect implausible commands online and retract them onto learned behaviors, so the robot attempts tasks in a "best-effort" manner. The result is a unified policy that takes real-time command, interacts with environment, and stays robust to implausible commands, falls, and disturbances. GigaBrain-WBC-0.5 achieves the highest success rate across all four regimes among three large-scale tracker baselines: 81.3% on terrain interaction (4.3x the strongest baseline), 83.1% under implausible commands, and 99.3% fall recovery (16.8x the strongest baseline). Hardware trials show robust interaction under missing supports and disturbances; the Unitree G1 checkpoint transfers to the Maker L01 robot with simple fine-tuning.

</details>


### [88] [GAPL: Grounded Action-effect Policy Learning for LLM-Based Trajectory Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18254)
*Zhihong Cui,Hengyu Liu,Zhangkai Wu,Yushuai Li,Tianyi Li,Peiyuan Guan,Amir Taherkordi,Tor Skeie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GAPL提出一个将LLM效果估计、仿真效果锚定与策略优化统一到闭环系统中的轨迹规划框架。包含LLM效果评估器、基于仿真的效果锚定器和效果感知决策器三个模块，在四个Highway-env场景中显著优于基线，降低了碰撞率、平均位移误差和最终位移误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在自动驾驶轨迹规划中具有语义丰富的规划能力，但受限于幻觉推理、对环境动态的锚定不足以及数值控制精度有限，需要一种统一框架来融合LLM的高层推理与精确的低层控制。

Method: 提出GAPL框架，包含三个模块：(1) LLM-based效果评估器，用于结构化多维动作效果估计；(2) 基于仿真的效果锚定器，从模拟器rollout预测动力学一致的效果；(3) 效果感知决策器，通过蒸馏器将LLM效果估计与仿真锚定，引导PPO策略学习。

Result: 在四个Highway-env场景中，GAPL一致优于基线，平均降低碰撞率0.76、ADE 0.86、FDE 2.00，平均奖励增益1.44。

Conclusion: 通过将LLM的语义推理能力与仿真环境的精确动力学锚定相结合，GAPL有效弥补了LLM在轨迹规划中的不足，实现高层推理与低层控制的统一。

Abstract: Trajectory planning for autonomous driving requires both high-level reasoning and precise low-level control. Large Language Models (LLMs) offer semantic-rich planning capabilities, however, their application is limited by hallucinated reasoning, poor grounding in environment dynamics, and limited numerical precision in control. We propose GAPL (Grounded Action-effect Policy Learning), a unified framework that integrates LLM-based effect estimation, simulation-based effect grounding, and policy optimization into a closed-loop system. GAPL consists of three modules: (1) an LLM-based Effect Evaluator for structured multi-dimensional action-effect estimation; (2) a Simulation-based Effect Grounder that predicts dynamics-consistent effects from simulator rollouts; and (3) an Effect-Aware Decision Maker that grounds LLM effect estimates against simulation via a distiller to guide Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO)-based policy learning. Experiments on four Highway-env scenarios demonstrate that GAPL consistently outperforms baselines, achieving average reductions of {0.76, 0.86, 2.00} in collision rate, average displacement error (ADE), and final displacement error (FDE), and an average reward gain of 1.44.

</details>


### [89] [VERAGMIL: Virtual Environment for Scooping Granular Foods with Imitation Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18258)
*Amanuel Ergogo,Diego Dall'Alba,Przemyslaw Korzeniowski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VERAGMIL是一个结合高保真模拟器与虚拟现实接口的机器人辅助喂食训练框架，用于采集颗粒状食物（米、豆类）操作演示数据，并支持多种模仿学习方法训练。在颗粒舀取和运输任务上评估了BC、BC-RNN、BCQ三种模型，结果显示VR演示显著优于3D空间鼠标，BCQ表现最佳，接近人类水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人辅助喂食（RAF）对残障人士至关重要，但操作颗粒状食物（如米饭、豆类）因其动态物理特性极具挑战性。从人类演示中学习是有效途径，但获取高质量演示数据困难，需要构建一个能高效采集演示数据并支持训练的系统。

Method: 构建VERAGMIL框架，包含高保真模拟器（含机器人、传感器、多种具有不同物理特性的食品）和直观的VR接口用于录制演示。在颗粒舀取和运输任务上，使用VR接口和3D空间鼠标两种方式采集演示数据，训练BC、BC-RNN、BCQ三种模仿学习模型，并与人类专家基线对比。

Result: VR演示数据显著优于3D空间鼠标数据；BCQ模型在总体性能上最优，尤其在减少溢出方面表现出色，接近人类专家水平；模型在成功率、溢出量、对未见食品的泛化能力和任务完成时间四个方面进行了评估。

Conclusion: VERAGMIL框架有效支持RAF系统在颗粒材料处理任务上的训练，VR接口采集的演示数据能显著提升模仿学习性能，为该领域提供了实用的开源解决方案。

Abstract: Robot-Assisted Feeding (RAF) systems are essential for assisting individuals with disabilities or motor impairments in eating tasks. Manipulating granular food items, such as rice and beans, poses significant challenges due to their dynamic physical properties. Learning from human demonstrations offers a promising solution, but acquiring high-quality demonstrations is complex. To address this, we present VERAGMIL, a framework that combines a high-fidelity simulator with an intuitive Virtual Reality (VR) interface for recording demonstrations and supporting different imitation learning methods. VERAGMIL provides a realistic environment for training RAF systems to handle granular materials, including robots, sensors, and various food items with distinct physical characteristics. We evaluate VERAGMIL by training three imitation learning models, BC, BC-RNN, and BCQ, on granular scooping and transporting tasks using both VR interface and 3D space mouse demonstrations, comparing them with a human-expert baseline. The models are assessed on success rate, spillage, generalization to unseen food items, and task completion time. Results show that VR-based demonstrations significantly outperform 3D space mouse data, with BCQ achieving the best overall performance, particularly in reducing spillage and approaching human performance. These findings underscore the effectiveness of our framework for training RAF systems in granular material handling. The code for our framework is publicly available at: https://github.com/AmanuelErgogo/VERAGMIL.git.

</details>


### [90] [Transferable Tool-Tissue Contact Detection from Stereo Depth in Robot-Assisted Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18270)
*Mingyeung Wu,Zhonghao Zhang,Hao Yang,Alan Kuntz,Jie Ying Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出利用深度图像中工具-组织最小距离（经-log10|d|变换）作为手术机器人中工具-组织接触检测的强迁移性线索，通过全监督两状态隐马尔可夫模型（HMM）建模，在六折LOSO训练、四组留出数据（跨任务跨对象）上达到宏F1 0.927、AUPRC 0.980，显著优于基于RGB的复现基线（跨类别宏F1仅0.320）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于RGB外观的工具-组织接触分类器泛化能力差，难以在不同任务和不同组织/模型上迁移，需要更鲁棒且物理可解释的接触检测线索。

Method: 利用立体视觉生成的深度图，在每帧中围绕工具边界定位空间支持的最近距离patch，将其归约为标量-log10|d|（距离越小信号越高）。用全监督两状态HMM建模该信号的上升/下降与接触状态的对应关系，以六折leave-one-session-out集成拟合（单个硅胶杯状模型），决策阈值由池化的out-of-fold预测选取。

Result: 在四个留出session（包含同任务同模型、同任务不同模型、不同任务不同模型三类）上达到宏F1 0.927、AUPRC 0.980；对比RGB基线在首类F1 0.965但在后两类大幅下降，跨四session宏F1仅0.320，表明深度距离信号是强迁移性的接触检测线索。

Conclusion: 工具-组织距离是比RGB外观更可迁移的接触检测特征，基于深度的最小距离信号可支撑手术机器人的交互感知控制与力估计。

Abstract: Reliable tool--tissue contact detection can support interaction-aware control and downstream force estimation in robot-assisted surgery. Most existing methods learn a contact classifier from RGB appearance, which is hard to generalize. In this work, we use the depth image generated from a stereo pair to give more information about tool--tissue contact. For each depth frame, we localize a spatially supported minimum-distance patch around the tool boundary and reduce it to a single scalar, $-\log_{10}|d|$; this signal rises and falls in step with ground-truth contact. We formalize this observation with a fully supervised two-state hidden Markov model. We fit this model as a six-fold leave-one-session-out (LOSO) ensemble on six palpation sessions against a single silicone cup-like phantom, with the decision threshold selected from the pooled out-of-fold predictions. It is evaluated on four held-out sessions of three categories: 1. same task on same phantom; 2. same task on different phantom; 3. different task on different phantom. This model reaches held-out macro F1 $0.927$ and AUPRC $0.980$. We further compare against a reproduction of an RGB-based contact classifier from prior work. This RGB-based model achieves high performance on the first category (F1 $0.965$), but substantially lower performance on the other two, resulting in macro F1 $0.320$ across all four sessions. These results indicate that the tool--tissue distance is a strong, transferable cue for contact detection in robot-assisted surgery.

</details>


### [91] [GuideFetch: A Task Coordination Framework for Concurrent Navigation and Object Retrieval in Assistive Robot Dogs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18292)
*Qian Yin,Ruiping Liu,Kunyu Peng,Jianxiang Man,Isik Baran Sandan,Junwei Zheng,Yufan Chen,Di Wen,Kailun Yang,Rainer Stiefelhagen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GuideFetch，一个用于异构导盲/取物双机器人团队并发导航与物体取回任务的协调框架。LLM从自然语言指令实例化调度条件化的四动作模式，在执行前对机器人、技能和目标别名进行归一化并验证动作合法性，随后基于机器人/物体状态执行串行或并行调度。在90种场景×种子组合的2x2研究中（360次执行），LLM计划100%通过验证；串并行分别达到72/90和71/90操作成功率，并行将平均makespan缩短41.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的辅助机器人任务（如导盲犬引导用户就座、另一只机器狗同时取咖啡）需要并发执行导航和物体取回，但语法合法的LLM计划可能违反embodiment约束，且看似成功的控制器运动并不能证明任务完成。现有方法缺乏在异构机器人团队中验证并协调并发调度的手段。

Method: GuideFetch框架包含：1) LLM从自然语言指令实例化一个调度条件化的四动作模式；2) 执行前归一化机器人、技能、目标别名，并对照已注册目标、机器人能力和所选调度验证动作；3) 根据机器人和物体状态控制顺序/并行执行。通过匹配的2x2研究（planner来源×调度模式）在90个场景-种子组合上进行360次执行评估。

Result: 所有180个在线LLM响应均无回退或重放通过验证，且与脚本化计划匹配。串行与并行执行分别获得72/90和71/90操作成功率。在56个两种调度均完成的任务中，并行执行将平均makespan降低41.3%。

Conclusion: 角色专业化与动作重叠能缩短已完成任务的时间，而状态检查能将计划有效性与验证过的任务完成区分开来。该工作在受控环境下验证了异构机器人团队并发任务的可行性与效率提升。

Abstract: Consider a robot guide dog escorting a blind user to an available seat while a second assistive robot dog concurrently retrieves a cup of coffee and delivers it to the same seat. This setting motivates concurrent execution because navigation and object retrieval can overlap. A syntactically valid Large Language Model (LLM) plan may still violate embodiment constraints, and successful-looking controller motion does not by itself establish task completion. We introduce \textsc{GuideFetch}, a coordination framework for concurrent navigation and object retrieval by a heterogeneous guider and fetcher team. An LLM instantiates a schedule-conditioned four-action schema from a natural-language instruction. Before execution, robot, skill, and target aliases are normalized, and proposed actions are validated against registered targets, robot capabilities, and the selected schedule. Robot and object states then govern sequential and parallel execution. In a matched $2\times2$ study across 90 combinations of scene and seed (360 executions), all 180 online LLM responses validate without fallback or replay and match the corresponding scripted plans. For each planner source, sequential and parallel execution achieve $72/90$ and $71/90$ operational successes, respectively. Among the 56 cases completed by both schedules, parallel execution reduces mean makespan by 41.3\%. Within this controlled setting, role specialization and action overlap shorten completed missions, while state checks distinguish plan validity from verified mission completion. Source code will be available.

</details>


### [92] [A Task-Agnostic Control Strategy for Dynamic Assistance with Pneumatically Actuated Soft Exosuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18364)
*Anoush Sepehri,Zachary Huang,Raymond de Callafon,Michael T. Tolley,Tania K. Morimoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种仅依赖运动学传感的逆系统控制策略，用于气动软体外骨骼，通过Hammerstein动态模型（Preisach迟滞+线性时不变滤波器）进行用户个性化建模（140秒数据），实现任务无关的动态辅助。测试台上交互扭矩减少高达73%，关键肌肉激活减少47%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气动人工肌肉为上肢软体外骨骼在康复、增强和日常生活辅助方面提供了新机会，但其复杂动力学和有限带宽使得基于用户意图的响应式辅助成为长期挑战。现有方法依赖生理或力传感器来解读用户意图，这限制了其在日常生活中的实用性。

Method: 采用Hammerstein动态模型描述人-机器人系统，该模型由Preisach迟滞模型（表征静态行为）和线性时不变滤波器（表征动态行为）组成。用140秒数据对每个用户进行个性化建模，通过求逆模型集成到控制环路中，仅依赖运动学传感实现任务无关的辅助控制。

Result: 在模拟腕部软辅助外骨骼的测试平台上，与无辅助条件相比，控制器将交互扭矩降低高达73%，关键屈肌和伸肌激活降低高达47%，速度范围为8°/s至120°/s。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一种无需生理或力传感器即可解读用户意图的控制策略，能为气动软体外骨骼提供任务无关的动态辅助，展示了气动软体外骨骼在日常生活中的实用潜力。

Abstract: Pneumatic artificial muscles have provided new opportunities to develop upper-extremity soft exosuits for reha- bilitation, augmentation, and assisted daily living. However, the complex dynamics and limited bandwidth of these actuators has made providing responsive assistance based on user intention a longstanding challenge. In this work, we present an inverse-plant control strategy for pneumatically actuated soft exosuits that only relies on kinematic sensing for task-agnostic and dynamic assistance during daily living. We model the human-robot system using a Hammerstein dynamic model, consisting of a Preisach hysteresis model and a linear time-invariant filter, to capture the static and dynamic behavior of the system. We personalize our model to each user using 140 s of data and approximate an inverse to integrate into our control loop. When evaluated on a test rig that emulated a soft assistive exosuit for the wrist, our controller reduced the interaction torque by up to 73% and the activation of key flexor and extensor muscles by up to 47% relative to the condition with no assistance for speeds ranging from 8°/s to 120°/s. Overall, this work presents a control strategy that can provide task-agnostic, dynamic assistance with pneumatically actuated soft exosuits without the need for physiological or force sensors to interpret user intention.

</details>


### [93] [The Embodiment Gap in Robot Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18433)
*Yukiyasu Domae,Keisuke Shirai,Hanbit Oh,Ryoichi Nakajo,Tomohiro Motoda,Koshi Makihara,Masaki Murooka,Takuma Yagi,Yoshiaki Bando,Ryo Hanai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"具身鸿沟"（embodiment gap）概念，指可复用模型/表征/数据与其在目标机器人上执行之间的差距。文章系统梳理跨具身复用研究中哪些可共享、哪些仍需在新型机器人上实现，用二维映射图归类现有方法，并提出一个超越成功率指标的适配工作报告框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人基础模型（RFMs/VLA）常以"规模扩展"视角被讨论，但现实中模型即使泛化良好，仍然需针对特定机器人本体做额外适配工作，不同方法所需适配量差异巨大且影响实际部署，而现有评测（如成功率）无法揭示这些差异，缺少系统性的分析框架。

Method: 提出"具身鸿沟"概念，构建两轴映射图（共享结构类型 × 执行适配所需阶段）分类现有方法，并从三个重叠研究方向整理文献：语义与感知共享、机器人数据与接口共享、跨具身对应关系学习；最后提出适配工作报告框架，明确跨具身学习中应核查的工作项及新机器人上仍需完成的工作。

Result: 给出了一套系统性的跨具身学习分类学和方法图谱，识别出可复用与不可复用的组件边界，并提出一个超越成功率指标的适配工作评估与报告框架，为跨具身研究和部署提供参考依据。

Conclusion: 跨具身学习不应只关注泛化精度，还需量化与报告部署到新本体时的适配工作量；"具身鸿沟"框架有助于统一评估标准、指导后续研究聚焦真正减少适配负担的方向。

Abstract: Robot foundation models (RFMs), including vision-language-action (VLA) policies, are often discussed through a scaling view: more data, larger models, and broader benchmarks should improve generalization. In robotics, however, a model can generalize while work still remains before it can run on a robot with a particular body. The work required differs across methods and target robots, and those differences affect practical deployment. We call the gap between reusable models, representations, or data and their use in execution on the target robot the embodiment gap. This survey examines what can be reused across robot embodiments and what must still be implemented on a new robot. We place existing methods on a two-axis map that shows the type of shared structure and the stage at which adaptation is needed for execution on the target robot. We then examine recent work through three overlapping research directions: sharing semantics and perception, sharing robot data and interfaces, and learning correspondence across embodiments. We also propose a reporting framework for adaptation work that success rate alone does not reveal. The framework identifies the work that should be checked when comparing cross-embodiment learning and highlights work that remains on a new robot and questions for future study.

</details>


### [94] [HarvestPoint-ACT: Explicit Target Selection and Harvest-Point Conditioning for Robotic Fruit Harvesting under Occlusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18446)
*Hanying Hu,Weipeng Li,Yikun Huang,Hao Chen,Zhengtao Hu,Changcai Yang,Weiwei Wan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HarvestPoint-ACT是一个端到端模仿学习框架，通过将决策显式化（实例分割前端预测每个水果的遮罩和收获点，调度器按遮挡和旅行距离排序选择目标），并结合动作分块Transformer处理抓取任务。在遮挡环境下模拟冠层实验中达到88%成功率，重度遮挡下75%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端模仿学习在果实采摘任务中面临两个关键问题：遮挡会导致策略丢失已选中的果实目标，且仅从像素中难以推断正确的抓取闭合点。作者希望通过将选择决策和抓取点预测显式化来缓解这些问题。

Method: 方法包含三个组件：(1) 实例分割前端+关键点分支，为每个可见果实预测mask和harvest point（抓取器闭合位置）；(2) 调度器按遮挡程度和旅行距离对候选果实排序并选择单个目标，每次尝试后重新检测和重新排序（因为冠层可能变化）；(3) 选中的果实被编码为八维状态（绝对收获点、抓取器到目标点的向量、有效性标志、置信度分数）输入action chunking transformer。若选中的果实暂时未被检测到，系统保留上一帧在机器人基座坐标系中的估计并标记为stale，若持续丢失则中止尝试。

Result: 在冠层模拟装置上，HarvestPoint-ACT达到88%成功率，重度遮挡下75%成功率。

Conclusion: 将感知决策（选择目标和抓取点）从策略中显式分离出来，通过实例分割和关键点检测替代纯像素推断，有效提升果实采摘在遮挡条件下的成功率。

Abstract: End-to-end imitation learning avoids hand-made robot motion for approaching and grasping, but the policy must still decide which fruit to pick and where to close the gripper. Occlusion can make the policy lose the selected fruit during harvesting, and the correct closing point is difficult to infer from pixels alone. This paper presents HarvestPoint-ACT, which makes both decisions explicit in perception and provides them to the policy. An instance segmentation front end with a keypoint branch predicts a mask and a harvest point for each visible fruit, where the harvest point specifies the location to close the gripper. A scheduler ranks detected candidates by occlusion and travel distance and selects one target. After each attempt, it redetects and reranks the candidates because the canopy may have changed. The selected fruit is encoded for an action chunking transformer as an eight-dimensional state, containing the absolute harvest point, the vector from the gripper to that point, a validity flag, and a confidence score. When the selected fruit is temporarily undetected, the system retains the last harvest point estimate in the robot base frame and marks it as stale, and aborts the attempt if the loss persists. On a canopy mock-up, HarvestPoint-ACT achieves a success rate of 88%, and of 75% under heavy occlusion.

</details>


### [95] [Backward Layout Search for Sequence-Constrained Robotic Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18454)
*Xi Zhang,Jiancong Dai,Hao Chen,Zhengtao Hu,Changcai Yang,Weiwei Wan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了Backward Layout Search (BLS)方法，用于机器人装配布局规划。它逆向装配顺序为每个零件分配初始位姿，通过几何、运动学、抓取和预定义运动检查，结合碰撞掩码和候选集过滤，在装配前即确保后续步骤的可执行性。在五个装配模型上验证了比正向搜索更高效、能生成无碰撞可执行布局。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人装配布局规划需要在执行给定装配序列时，确定装配站点和每个零件的初始位姿。难点在于每个装配步骤后障碍环境变化，未装配零件会阻塞机器人运动，使得布局规划问题极具挑战性。

Method: 核心洞察：每个装配步骤的可行性仅取决于当前及后续装配零件的初始位姿。基于此依赖关系，BLS按逆装配顺序分配初始零件位姿。每次扩展执行几何、运动学、抓取和预定义运动检查，通过碰撞掩码和候选集过滤移除不可行的初始位姿候选。光束搜索保留有希望的局部布局，最终用完整正向装配顺序的运动规划验证完整布局。

Result: 在五个装配模型上的实验表明，BLS能生成无碰撞可执行布局，相比匹配的正向搜索减少了步骤评估次数和搜索时间。

Conclusion: BLS验证了逆序布局搜索的有效性，通过利用装配步骤间的依赖关系，显著提升了装配布局规划的效率和可行性。

Abstract: Robotic assembly layout planning must determine the assembly site and the initial pose of each part while ensuring collision-free execution of a prescribed assembly sequence. This problem is challenging because the obstacle environment changes after each assembly step, and unassembled parts re maining in the workspace may block robot motions. We observe that the feasibility of each assembly step depends only on the initial poses of the current and later-assembled parts. Based on this dependency, we propose Backward Layout Search (BLS), which assigns initial part poses in reverse assembly order. Each expansion performs geometric, kinematic, grasp, and prescribed-motion checks, while collision masks and candidate set filtering remove infeasible initial part pose candidates. Promising partial layouts are retained through beam selection, and complete layouts are validated by full motion planning in forward assembly order. Experiments on five assembly models show that BLS produces collision-free executable layouts and reduces step evaluations and search time compared with a matched forward search.

</details>


### [96] [DevGRU: Depth-guided Visual Navigation using a Collision-aware Recurrent Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18470)
*Kyung Min Han,Eunsom Kim,Young J. Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DevGRU，一个基于深度图像和点目标条件的导航系统，通过动作预测器生成碰撞感知的未来轨迹，结合碰撞预测器补偿目标姿态估计误差，在九种场景和多种基线对比中大幅超越ViNT和NoMaD，同时模型参数量和推理速度均具显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉导航基础模型在复杂室内环境（结构化布局和狭窄通道）中容易发生碰撞，影响部署可靠性。需要解决导航模型在复杂室内环境中的避障能力和误差补偿问题。

Method: DevGRU采用深度图像与点目标条件作为输入，核心组件包括：（1）动作预测器（AP），生成碰撞感知的未来轨迹以有效避让即时障碍；（2）碰撞预测器，与AP协同补偿目标姿态估计中累积的误差，并主动缓解未来偏差。

Result: 在九种场景中对比ViNT、NoMaD、NavDP三种SOTA方法及四种变体，DevGRU在导航性能上大幅超越ViNT和NoMaD；可训练参数量少，推理速度最快，相比NavDP模型大小缩减7倍、推理时间加速17倍。

Conclusion: DevGRU通过轻量级设计和碰撞感知轨迹生成，在复杂室内导航中实现了高性能与高效率的平衡，为嵌入式/轻量级导航模型提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Existing visual navigation models often aim to develop foundation models that can generalize robot navigation across diverse platforms. However, many of these models are prone to collisions when deployed in complex indoor environments, particularly in structured layouts and narrow passages. To address this problem, we propose a depth image- and point-goal-conditioned navigation system, DevGRU. The proposed system employs an action predictor (AP) that generates collision-aware future trajectories, enabling effective avoidance of immediate obstacles. In conjunction with a collision predictor, the AP further compensates for errors accumulated in the goal pose estimation and proactively mitigates future deviations. To evaluate our method, we conducted experiments across nine different scenes and three state-of-the-art approaches - ViNT, NoMaD, and NavDP - as well as four additional variants of ViNT and NoMaD. In terms of navigation performance, DevGRU significantly outperforms ViNT and NoMaD by a large margin. In addition, the proposed model has a relatively small number of trainable parameters, resulting in the fastest inference time among the baselines, particularly outperforming NavDP by 7x in model size and 17x in inference time.

</details>


### [97] [An Experimental Study of Downwash Effects on a Continuum Manipulator Integrated with a Multirotor UAV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18507)
*Anuraj Uthayasooriyan,Krishna Manaswi Digumarti,ernando Vanegas,Felipe Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文实验研究了多旋翼平台下方桨下洗流对绳驱连续体机械臂（CM）运动学偏差的影响，量化了自由空间四档油门、近墙和近地下最大油门下的末端位姿偏差，并学习了一个常曲率引导的高斯过程回归（GPR）残差模型，将前向位姿预测的RMSE分别降低了89-95%（位置）和47-79%（姿态）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体机械臂气动操作虽具柔顺性和灵巧性优势，但旋翼下洗流会显著影响其在受限或危险环境中的性能，尤其是近墙和近地面场景。该效应此前对连续体机械臂尚未被表征，亟需实验量化以支持精确建模与补偿。

Method: 将绳驱连续体机械臂集成于多旋翼平台，静风条件下与常曲率（CC）模型对比；随后在自由空间四档油门、最大油门近墙和近地条件下，量化下洗引起的末端位姿偏差（相对静风实验基线）；最后用CC引导的GPR残差模型学习实验数据，提升前向位姿预测。

Result: 垂直位置和偏航角偏差最大，且地面效应会放大这些偏差。CC引导的GPR残差模型显著提升前向位姿预测精度（位置RMSE改善89-95%，姿态RMSE改善47-79%），支持面向补偿的下洗感知连续体机械臂气动操作建模。

Conclusion: 该工作首次表征了连续体机械臂在下洗流下的运动学偏差，提出了一种数据驱动的下洗感知残差模型，为后续基于补偿的连续体臂气动操作系统设计提供基础。

Abstract: Continuum arm aerial manipulation systems leverage soft-manipulator compliance and dexterity for tasks in confined or hazardous environments, but propeller downwash can degrade performance, particularly near walls and the ground. This effect remains uncharacterized for continuum manipulators. This letter experimentally studies downwash-induced kinematic deviations of a tendon-driven continuum manipulator integrated with a multirotor platform. Under still-air conditions, the CM is compared with a constant-curvature (CC) model. Downwash- induced end-effector pose deviations are then quantified relative to the mean still-air experimental baseline at four propeller throttle levels in free space, and at maximum throttle near a wall, and near the ground. Vertical position and yaw show the largest deviations and are amplified by ground effect. A CC-guided Gaussian process regression (GPR) residual model is learned from experimental data that improves forward pose prediction RMSE (position by 89-95%, orientation by 47-79%), and support compensation-oriented, downwash-aware modeling of continuum arm aerial manipulation systems.

</details>


### [98] [Real-Time Control-Constrained DDP for Underactuated Balancing of Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18552)
*SeongWon Nam,Hyunyong Lee,Hansol Kang,Jiman Park,Yeongwoo Son,Bumsu Yi,Jaeyoung Oh,Hyouk Ryeol Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于加速梯度投影(APG)的控制约束DDP框架(ABC-DDP)，用于欠驱动腿式机器人的实时模型预测控制(MPC)，通过虚拟约束整合控制约束于可行性驱动的多重射击框架中，首次在统一MPC框架下实现了四足机器人静态双腿站立、慢速猫步、直立行走和高速奔跑等多种动态运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典DDP方法在处理控制约束方面存在局限，而实际腿式机器人控制中控制约束（如力矩/力限制）至关重要。此外，现有方法难以在欠驱动条件下实现从动力学不可行初始状态出发的稳定优化，以及同时支持静态站立和多种动态运动。

Method: 提出ABC-DDP框架：1) 采用加速投影梯度(APG)方法高效求解控制约束DDP，无需反复进行KKT矩阵求逆即可识别活动集；2) 引入虚拟约束将控制约束整合到可行性驱动的多重射击框架中，保证从动力学不可行的初始化仍能稳定优化；3) 支持短时域、实时MPC，适用于强欠驱动系统。

Result: 仿真结果显示：在外部扰动下实现静态双腿站立；在统一MPC框架下实现慢速猫步、直立行走和高速奔跑等多样化动态运动。据作者所述，这是首次通过实时有限时域MPC实现四足机器人静态双腿站立。

Conclusion: ABC-DDP有效解决了经典DDP在控制约束下的计算效率和稳定性问题，为腿式机器人的统一实时MPC框架提供了新方案，并首次在统一框架下实现了静态站立与多种动态运动的结合。

Abstract: This paper presents a real-time control-constrained Differential Dynamic Programming (DDP) framework for underactuated legged robots. To address the limitation of classical DDP in handling control constraints, we propose an Accelerated Projected Gradient (APG)-based control-constrained DDP (ABC-DDP), which efficiently computes constrained solutions and identifies active sets without repeated Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) inversions. A virtual constraint is introduced to integrate control constraints within a feasibility-driven multiple-shooting framework, enabling stable optimization even from dynamically infeasible initializations. The proposed method supports real-time model predictive control (MPC) with short horizons under strong underactuation. Simulation results demonstrate static two-leg standing under external disturbances, along with diverse dynamic motions including slow catwalk, upright walking, and high-speed running within a unified MPC framework. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first demonstration of static two-leg standing of a quadruped robot achieved using real-time finite-horizon MPC.

</details>


### [99] [LabDex: A Hierarchical Benchmark for Dexterous Manipulation in Laboratories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18618)
*Zhipeng Tang,Sihang Chen,Sha Zhang,Peihao Yang,Yan Liu,Wentao Zhao,Xinrui Liu,Rui Huang,Wensheng Du,Yuting Huang,Jiajun Deng,Lidian Wang,Yuan Zhang,Yanyong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LabDex是一个大规模真实世界数据集与基准，用于化学实验室中的灵巧操作，它首次在统一框架下联合了真实世界与仿真平台，并通过三级任务层级（原子技能、组合技能、长时程实验工作流）系统地组织实验室操作，支持跨层级评估机器人学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主实验室需要机器人灵巧地操作各种实验器具并执行长时程、状态依赖的实验流程，但现有基准无法同时捕捉灵巧手使用、真实实验室交互和多阶段实验流程，限制了系统的训练与评估。

Method: 设计了LabDex数据集与基准：跨平台统一真实与仿真环境（共同框架提供标准化任务定义、演示数据与评估协议），并围绕三级层级分类法组织——原子技能（基础灵巧操作）、组合技能和长时程实验工作流，支持端任务评估及技能组合与影响的分析。

Result: 在真实与仿真环境中对代表性机器人学习方法进行了跨层级评估，实验结果验证了LabDex任务设计与演示数据的有效性，表明该基准能支持现有机器人策略在不同层级实验室灵巧操作任务上的训练与系统评估。

Conclusion: LabDex为自主实验室机器人的训练与系统评估提供了基础平台，填补了灵巧操作、真实实验室交互与多阶段实验流程联合基准的空白，为后续研究与开发奠定基础。

Abstract: Autonomous laboratories hold great promise for accelerating scientific discovery. To achieve this vision, robots are supposed to dexterously manipulate diverse labware and instruments and execute long-horizon, state-dependent experimental procedures. Yet existing benchmarks do not jointly capture dexterous hand use, real-world laboratory interactions, and multi-stage experimental procedures, limiting systematic training and evaluation. To bridge this gap, we introduce LabDex, a large-scale real-world dataset and benchmark for dexterous manipulation in chemistry laboratories, organized around a hierarchical task taxonomy spanning atomic skills, compositional tasks, and long-horizon experiments. First, LabDex is cross-platform and, for the first time, unifies real-world and simulation platforms under a common framework, providing standardized task definitions, demonstrations, and evaluation protocols. Second, LabDex is large-scale and systematically organizes chemistry laboratory operations into three interconnected levels: Atomic Skills, which characterize fundamental dexterous manipulation capabilities; Compositional Skills; and Long-Horizon Laboratory Workflows. This hierarchical design not only supports the evaluation of end-task performance, but also enables the analysis of how fundamental dexterous skills compose and influence more complex laboratory operations. We conduct cross-level evaluations of representative robot learning methods in both real-world and simulation environments. The experimental results validate the effectiveness of the LabDex task design and demonstration data, and show that the benchmark supports the training and systematic evaluation of existing robotic policies across laboratory dexterous manipulation tasks at different levels, providing a foundation for further research and development of autonomous laboratory robots.

</details>


### [100] [Evaluation of Monocular SLAM Systems on High-Altitude Nadir UAV Footage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18632)
*Gašper Spagnolo,Matej Dobrevski,Danijel Skočaj*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文系统评估了五种单目SLAM系统在无人机航拍俯视视频场景下的表现。结果显示MASt3R-SLAM在DJI五组飞行数据上取得最低平均水平MAE（参考路径长度的0.53%），而DROID-SLAM综合表现最佳（完成运行的平均误差为参考路径长度的2.88%）。但所有系统在长距离航拍序列上均无法保持全局轨迹形状，垂直方向定位精度始终较差，说明当前单目SLAM方法不足以单独支撑纯视觉空中导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机航拍视频具有弱几何约束和严重感知混叠的特点（俯视场景缺乏视差、相似地面纹理导致误匹配），对单目SLAM构成独特挑战。此前缺乏系统性的基准评测来量化当前SLAM方法在该场景下的实际能力上限，论文旨在填补这一空白，回答"现有单目SLAM是否足够支持纯视觉空中导航"这一关键问题。

Method: 论文构建了多尺度的基准测试集，包括五条DJI无人机实飞序列、合成城市级图像序列、以及长距离航拍序列（GES和ALTO）。在无惯性测量单元（IMU）和无全球导航卫星系统（GNSS）辅助的纯视觉条件下，对五种单目SLAM系统（含DROID-SLAM和MASt3R-SLAM）进行统一评测，以参考路径长度的百分比作为水平定位误差指标。

Result: 性能随环境和轨迹尺度变化显著：MASt3R-SLAM在DJI五条实飞数据上取得最低水平平均MAE（0.53%路径长度）；DROID-SLAM综合最优，平均误差2.88%路径长度；但所有系统在GES和ALTO长距离序列上均无法保持全局轨迹形状。垂直方向定位普遍较差，大区域轨迹即使在具有回环检测能力的情况下仍高度变形。

Conclusion: 当前单目SLAM方法在无人机俯视航拍场景下的纯视觉定位能力不足，无法可靠支撑无辅助的空中导航任务。该基准为后续研究指明了方向：需要针对航拍俯视特性（弱约束、高混叠、大尺度）设计专门的SLAM方法。

Abstract: Aerial nadir video combines weak geometric constraints with severe perceptual aliasing, making it a difficult regime for monocular SLAM. We benchmark five monocular SLAM systems on local UAV flights, synthetic city-scale imagery, and long-range aerial sequences. To isolate visual performance, we provide no inertial or GNSS aiding. Performance varies strongly with environment and trajectory scale: MASt3R-SLAM achieves the lowest mean horizontal MAE on the five DJI flights (0.53\% of reference path length), whereas no system consistently preserves global trajectory shape on the long GES and ALTO sequences. Overall, DROID-SLAM performs best, averaging 2.88\% of reference path length across completed runs. Vertical position remains poor, and large-area trajectories remain highly distorted despite loop-closure capability. Current monocular SLAM methods are by themselves therefore insufficient for reliable visual-only aerial navigation.

</details>


### [101] [Progressive Experience Fusion for Multi-Task World Model Control in Endovascular Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18647)
*Harry Robertshaw,Maxence Boels,Nikola Fischer,Sebastien Ourselin,Christos Bergeles,Alejandro Granados,Thomas C Booth*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出渐进式经验融合（PEF）方法训练多任务TD-MPC2控制器，用于自主血管内导航（机械取栓手术）。PEF在10个已知训练解剖结构上平均成功率74%，优于SAC（37%）和基线TD-MPC2（65%）；在30个血管结构上训练的PEF+自适应规划在10个全新血管结构上达到90%成功率，并在体外荧光透视环境下成功迁移至患者特异性血管模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主血管内导航可以支持机械取栓手术在医疗资源匮乏地区的推广，但控制器需要应对长距离、多阶段、跨不同血管解剖结构的导航任务。现有方法难以有效处理这种多任务、多解剖结构的复杂控制问题。

Method: 提出渐进式经验融合（PEF）训练策略，用于训练多任务TD-MPC2控制器；同时引入基于残差动作序列离散度的自适应MPPI规划时域启发式方法，并采用患者特异仿真进行微调。

Result: PEF在五个子任务、十个已知训练解剖结构中平均成功率74%（SAC为37%，p<0.001；TD-MPC2为65%，p=0.053）。30个血管训练+自适应时域规划的PEF在10个未见血管结构中成功率90%。在体外未见过卒中患者血管模型上成功迁移，微调后路径比从63%提升至80%（p<0.001）。

Conclusion: 该工作为多血管结构训练和患者特异性自适应提供了概念验证，展示自主血管内导航的技术可行性，但仍需进一步验证才能进入临床部署。

Abstract: Autonomous endovascular navigation could support the delivery of mechanical thrombectomy to underserved areas, but controllers must navigate long, multi-stage paths across varying vascular anatomies. This study investigates Progressive Experience Fusion (PEF) to train a multi-task TD-MPC2 controller. We additionally evaluate a heuristic that changes the Model Predictive Path Integral planning horizon using residual action-sequence dispersion, and fine-tuning in a patient-specific simulation. Across five subtasks in ten known training anatomies with held-out targets, PEF achieved a mean success rate of 74%, compared with 37% for Soft Actor-Critic (p < 0.001) and 65% for base TD-MPC2 (p = 0.053). A PEF controller with adaptive-horizon planning trained on 30 vasculatures achieved a mean success rate of 90% in ten held-out vasculatures. The PEF agent successfully transferred to an unseen in vitro stroke patient vasculature under fluoroscopy, achieving a mean path ratio improvement from 63% to 80% with fine-tuning (p < 0.001), following 40x103 fine-tuning steps (corresponding to approximately 107 min of clinical inter-hospital transfer time). This work represents a proof of concept for multi-vasculature training and patient-specific adaptation, while further validation is required before clinical deployment.

</details>


### [102] [Orienteering Problem with Uncertain Time-Varying Rewards: Framework and Benchmark for Everyday Service Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18672)
*Masafumi Endo,Kohei Honda,Yuu Jinnai,Ryo Yonetani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出不确定时变奖励的定向越野问题（OP-UTVR），允许智能体从观测估计奖励动态并预测未来奖励，从而实现鲁棒路由决策。作者提出三种不同规划周期与在线自适应能力的规划器，给出随机奖励下的理论性能界，并引入一个移动服务机器人室内行人环境基准。实验揭示了规划周期与自适应之间的权衡，并证明了长周期规划+在线自适应的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有定向越野问题（OP）大多假设奖励预先已知，但实际应用中奖励往往不确定且随时间变化（如配送代理面临的客户需求波动），需要放松这一假设以支持更实际的路径规划。

Method: 提出OP-UTVR问题形式化；设计了三种不同规划周期和在线自适应程度的规划器；推导了奖励随机性条件下各规划器的理论性能界；构建了移动服务机器人在室内环境中导航行人的基准。

Result: 实验展示了规划周期与在线自适应之间的权衡，并证明长周期规划结合在线自适应策略在不确定时变奖励场景下表现有效。

Conclusion: OP-UTVR为不确定时变奖励下的路径规划提供了一个新的问题框架和求解方法，长周期规划与在线自适应的结合是应对动态奖励环境的有效策略。

Abstract: We present the orienteering problem with uncertain time-varying rewards (OP-UTVR), a novel variant of the orienteering problem (OP). While most existing OP formulations assume rewards to be known in advance, practical applications involve uncertain and time-varying rewards, as with shifting customer demand for delivery agents. OP-UTVR relaxes this assumption by allowing agents to estimate reward dynamics from observations and forecast future rewards. This enables informed routing decisions despite stochastic reward changes and inevitable prediction errors.
